        .type           _qsa_prefill_score_kernel,@function

        .other          _qsa_prefill_score_kernel,@"STO_CUDA_ENTRY STV_DEFAULT"
_qsa_prefill_score_kernel:
.text._qsa_prefill_score_kernel:
        /*0000*/                   LDC R1, c[0x0][0x28] ;
        /*0010*/                   S2UR UR5, SR_CTAID.X ;
        /*0020*/                   ULDC.64 UR6, c[0x0][0x228] ;
        /*0030*/                   ULDC.64 UR20, c[0x0][0x208] ;
        /*0040*/                   LDC.64 R2, c[0x0][0x228] ;
        /*0050*/                   S2R R22, SR_CTAID.Y ;
        /*0060*/                   ULDC UR8, c[0x0][0x258] ;
        /*0070*/                   ULEA UR4, UP0, UR5, UR6, 0x2 ;
        /*0080*/                   ULEA.HI.X UR6, UR5, UR7, URZ, 0x2, UP0 ;
        /*0090*/                   IMAD.U32 R4, RZ, RZ, UR4 ;
        /*00a0*/                   ULDC UR4, c[0x0][0x254] ;
        /*00b0*/                   LDG.E R2, desc[UR20][R2.64] ;
        /*00c0*/                   IMAD.U32 R5, RZ, RZ, UR6 ;
        /*00d0*/                   LDG.E R7, desc[UR20][R4.64] ;
